## Run

  source_paths = ["src/commerce_core/platform/", "src/commerce_core/accounts/"]
  pytest_add_cli_args_test_selection = ["tests/"]
  max-children = 1 (one shared test database; parallel children would collide on it)
  5 tests deselected because they read core's source files, which mutmut rewrites with trampolines:

    tests/static/test_release_tools.py::test_current_registry_parses_to_the_live_table
  An earlier run in a scratch copy without the deselection stopped in the stats phase: the AST
  test found 58 violations in the instrumented source. Not a product failure; recorded here.

## Caveat on 'no tests' and 'survived'

mutmut maps each function to the tests that ran it during its stats pass. Code that runs
in the session-scoped database setup (bootstrap_db, the release step, migrations' seed
functions), in other threads (concurrency tests, live_server) or in a subprocess (the worker)
is not attributed to the tests that depend on it. Some 'no tests' and 'survived' mutants in
those modules are therefore unexercised by mutmut, not necessarily untested. Nothing here
was verified mutant by mutant.
